- **test-open-space**: Checks a parcel's provided open space against the requirement in the zoning text you supply or paste, and does the part that goes wrong: applying the definition. Open space, usable open space, landscaped area, permeable area and amenity space are different terms with different rules about what counts, and a check that measures the wrong area passes on paper. ([kosif199022-jpg/kosif-atlas](https://github.com/kosif199022-jpg/kosif-atlas/blob/HEAD/skills/1120-ambient-library/2593-aimm-commands/commands/prompt-abstraction-ladder.md) · 3 copies)
- **The Quality Gate Rubric**: Force self-evaluation at every handoff: generate an artifact-specific rubric, score against it, revise up to 3 times until 9/10, or hand off with a flagged failure. The mechanism that made Lou's live Brand Writing Team build score 9s end-to-end (Apr 9, 2026). ([coachlou/ambient-library](https://github.com/coachlou/ambient-library/blob/HEAD/library/aimm-commands/commands/quality-gates.md))
- **The Self-Brief Planner**: Force Claude to think before it solves, surface the research, questions, decisions, and documents you actually need before diving into answers. From the May 28, 2026 AIMM session, Lou's reframe for Joanna: stop asking for answers one at a time and start by asking what you need to know. ([coachlou/ambient-library](https://github.com/coachlou/ambient-library/blob/HEAD/library/aimm-commands/commands/self-brief.md))
- **The Skeptic**: Structured adversarial review of any AI output — identifying vulnerable assumptions, simulating failure scenarios, and producing a hardened revision. The single highest-ROI post-generation habit — costs nothing on a subscription and routinely produces substantial improvements. ([kosif199022-jpg/kosif-atlas](https://github.com/kosif199022-jpg/kosif-atlas/blob/HEAD/skills/1120-ambient-library/2593-aimm-commands/commands/skeptic.md) · 3 copies)
- **The Source Lock**: Constrain Claude to a specific authoritative source, kills hallucinated citations by treating one URL as ground truth. From the May 28, 2026 AIMM session, Lou writing a CLAUDE.md instruction live to stop Claude from inventing regulatory citations in Joanna's legal documents. ([coachlou/ambient-library](https://github.com/coachlou/ambient-library/blob/HEAD/library/aimm-commands/commands/source-lock.md))
- **The Sourdough Excavator**: A Socratic positioning interview that uncovers what makes your expertise genuinely different — not your marketing claims, but the hard-won, specific, unteachable things you know that nobody else in your space knows they should be looking for. ([kosif199022-jpg/kosif-atlas](https://github.com/kosif199022-jpg/kosif-atlas/blob/HEAD/skills/1120-ambient-library/2593-aimm-commands/commands/sourdough-excavator.md) · 3 copies)
- **The Symptom Layer Discovery**: Map the gap between what you sell and what your clients actually experience before they know they need help — surfacing pre-awareness symptoms, client-language queries, and content blind spots. ([kosif199022-jpg/kosif-atlas](https://github.com/kosif199022-jpg/kosif-atlas/blob/HEAD/skills/1120-ambient-library/2593-aimm-commands/commands/symptom-layer.md) · 3 copies)
- **The Transcript Miner**: Extract high-signal nuggets from any conversation transcript — the non-obvious insights, quotable moments, actionable techniques, and content seeds that would take hours to find manually. Turns raw transcripts into structured intellectual capital. ([kosif199022-jpg/kosif-atlas](https://github.com/kosif199022-jpg/kosif-atlas/blob/HEAD/skills/1120-ambient-library/2593-aimm-commands/commands/transcript-miner.md) · 3 copies)
- **The Voice Profile Builder**: Construct a precise, reusable model of anyone's writing voice by analyzing the delta between their drafts and their edits — the gap between "what AI wrote" and "what I actually sound like" is the most information-dense signal for voice modeling. ([kosif199022-jpg/kosif-atlas](https://github.com/kosif199022-jpg/kosif-atlas/blob/HEAD/skills/1120-ambient-library/2593-aimm-commands/commands/voice-profile-builder.md) · 3 copies)
